# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 3856 files · ~9,939,656 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 89 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 28, .css 15, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 56936 nodes · 160509 edges · 1192 communities (1107 shown, 85 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5644 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `fc2edb8d`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
